JUDGMENT
These writ petitions are connected and therefore, I am
disposing these writ petitions by a common judgment.
2. I will narrate the facts in W.P.(C). No.6568/2018:
Petitioners approached this Court aggrieved by the
demand of property tax by respondents 2 and 3 regarding the
financial year starting from 2013-14 to 2016-17. According to
the petitioners, they remitted the entire property tax as
demanded by the Municipality at the relevant time for those
years. The petitioners remitted property tax as required by
law to the 2nd respondent Municipality till 2016-17 is the
submission. It is also the case of the petitioners that they are
ready to remit the property tax for the year 2017-18 as

demanded by respondents 2 and 3. It is also submitted that
most of the buildings owned by the petitioners are
accommodated either by their own business concerns or given
on rent to tenants. According to the petitioners, all the rooms
of the petitioners are occupied and various business activities
are being conducted legally, after obtaining valid
permission/license from respondents 2 and 3 as required
under the provisions of the Kerala Municipality Act and the
Rules. It is the case of the petitioners that the 2
nd respondent
Municipality has not adopted any resolution enhancing the
basic property tax rates with regard to the buildings, situated
within the municipal area after 2013. No resolution
whatsoever has been adopted to enhance the existing property
tax rate in the 2nd respondent Municipality. It is also the case
of the petitioners that no notice as required under Rule 4(2) of
the Rules was ever been published or notified by the
Municipality. No resolution as required under Section 233(3)
of the Act has been ever taken by the Municipality. In such
circumstances the demand is illegal is the contention. In some
of the writ petitions, the local authorities submitted that the

2. നിവിലു്ᩦ ലെ്ᨾി്ᨲൾ്ᨪ് ്ᩜി്ᩌ് ഏരിയ അടി്ᩝാന്ᩈിൽ
വᩜ᪎നികുതി പരി്ᩌരണം വരു്ᩈിയതിലെ# ്ᩚാബ്യ്ᩈീയതിയായി
നി്ᩐയി്ᨴിരിᨪ᪎്ᩐത് 01.04.2013 ആണ്. ആയതിനാൽ നഗരസഭൾ വᩜ᪎നികുതി
പരി്ᩌരണം നടᩈ᪎കേ്ᩘാൾ നികുതി പരി്ᩌരണ്ᩈിലെ# ഭാഗമായി മാ്ᩢം വരു്ᩐ
തുയും പിശയും 2013 ഏ്ᩚിൽ 1 മുതൽ ഈടാᨪ᪎ᩐ᪎്ᩂ് . നികുതി വർ്ᩍനവിലെ#
ഭാഗമായി വരു്ᩐ വർ്ᩍി്ᩔി്ᨴ തു 2013 ഏ്ᩚിൽ 1 ്ᩚാബ്യ്ᩈിൽ ഇകേ്ᩔാൾ
ഈടാᨪ᪎്ᩐതു ാരണം ലെപാതുജന്ᨲൾ്ᨪ് വിയ ബു്ᩍിമുᨾ᪎്ᩂാവു്ᩐ ാര്യം
്ᩦ്ᩍയിൽലെ്ᩔ്ᨾിᨾ᪎്ᩂ്. വിയ തു ഒ്ᩐി്ᨴ് അടകേ᪚❙്ᩂി വരു്ᩐത് പ ലെ്ᨾിട
ഉടമൾᨪ᪎ം സാ്ᩘ്ᩈി ്ᩚതിസ്ᩏി❙⟛ം ഇടയാകുᩐ᪎്ᩂ്.
3. കേരള മുനിസി്ᩔാി്ᩢി ആ്ᨴ് 539-ാാം വകു്ᩔ് ്ᩚാരം നഗരസഭ᪚❙്

ലൈവരി്ᨴ പുകേരാഗതി ബ്ᩏലെ്ᩔ്ᨾ നഗരസഭ ലെസ്ᩅ്ᨾറി ആ്ᩎ കേതാറും അവകോന
കേയാഗം കൂടി വിയിരുᩈ᪎യും, പുകേരാഗതി നഗരാര്യ ഡയറ്ᨴറുലെട ഓഫീസികേ്ᨪ്
എ്ᩤാ ലെവ്ᩦിയാ്ᩎയും ഇ ലെമയിൽ വഴി റികേ്ᩔാർ്ᨾ് ലെåകേ£്ᩂതുമാണ്. ഇ്ᨪാര്യ്ᩈിൽ
കേവ്ᩂ പുകേരാഗതി ലൈവരിᨪ᪎ᩐ᪎കേ്ᩂാലെയ്ᩐ് നഗരാര്യ ഡയറ്ᨴർ പരികേശാധി്ᨴ്
ഉറ്ᩔാകേ്ᨪ്ᩂതാണ് .
8. ഈ നടപടി്ᩅമ്ᨲൾ സംബ്ᩏി്ᨴ് കേരള മുനിസി്ᩔാി്ᩢി ലെ്ᨾിട
å്ᨾ്ᨲളിൽ കേവ്ᩂ കേഭദഗതി ലെå്ᩓ് വി്ᨺാപനം പുറലെ്ᩔടുവികേ്ᨪ്ᩂതാണ്.
(ഗവർ്ᩆറുലെട ഉ്ᩈരവിൽ ്ᩚാരം)
 ടി.ലെ. കേജാസ്
അഡീഷണൽ åീഫ് ലെസ്ᩅ്ᨾറി"
4. In the light of the above Government Order, it is
clear that the property tax can be reviewed only with effect
from 01.04.2016. If that be the case, the impugned orders in
these writ petitions can be set aside and the local authority
concerned can be allowed to take appropriate steps in

8926, 11090, 11102 & 13180 of 2020
62
accordance with Ext.R3(e). All other contentions raised by the
petitioners in these writ petitions can be left open.
Therefore, these writ petitions are disposed of in the
following manner:
1. In the light of Ext.R3(e) in W.P.(C). No.6568/2018
[G.O.(Rt) No.540/2019/LSGD dated 06.03.2019],
all the impugned demand notices and demand
are set aside.
2. Local authorities are free to take appropriate
steps in accordance with G.O.(Rt)
No.540/2019/LSGD dated 06.03.2019. If the
petitioners are aggrieved by the fresh demand
notice issued by the local authorities as per G.O.
(Rt) No.540/2019/LSGD dated 06.03.2019, they
are free to challenge the same in accordance
with law.
